# Open Threads

**HOW TO READ THIS FILE. Append-only; each new block restates the last.** **The authoritative block is *THE VOLUME 08 OUTLINE*, at the foot of this file, and it is the newest and it wins, and the next phase is `workspace/volume-08/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, which is a BATCH and not an outline phase. The block headed *THE VOLUME 07 CLOSE, AND ITS REVIEW REPAIR PASS* is above it and is history.** The block this header named until the review repair pass on the close phase moved it, *THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — the threads that are still open on the last page of the volume*, is history, as is *THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — no thread closed and no thread opened, and the next phase is a batch*, which named a phase that has run, and as is *THE VOLUME 06 CLOSE — four threads closed, two opened, and the next phase is an outline phase*, which is superseded for its five figures and for the page fault in Chapter 0300, which is now repaired, and which stands for everything else, including the four threads it closed and the two it opened, none of which the repair pass touched. **Everything older that disagrees with the newest block is superseded**, including *The Volume 06 outline phase — review repair pass*, including *Live into Volume 06, Batch 0001*, including *Live into the Volume 06 outline, after the review repair pass on the close phase*, including *Live into the Volume 06 outline*, including *Live into the Volume 05 close*, and including *Live into Volume 05, Batch 0006*, which named a sixth batch that would have written Chapters 0251 to 0260 and which the review repair pass deleted. **A repair pass closes no thread and opens none, and a repair pass that resolves a live thread is a worse fault than the one it fixes.**

Status after **the Volume 06 close** — Volume 06 is fifty chapters on fifty days, days 251 to 300, one chapter to a day, no double day, and **all fifty are on disk and the volume is CLOSED.** `state/volume-06-close.md` exists, it was rewritten and re-verified by the close phase against the fifty chapters rather than against the batch records, and it is the volume's inheritance and it replaces `state/volume-05-close.md` for anything that reads a volume's close record. **THE CLOSE PHASE WROTE NO CHAPTER AND DECIDED NOTHING: it re-measured the volume, corrected six published figures, found one page fault it was not allowed to fix, and handed the next decision to the next phase.** **Volume 06 is the first volume in this repository whose outline phase was allowed to decide and it is the first volume whose fifty chapters all paid its plan, and the four decisions of record that made it possible are in `outline/series.md` under *DECISIONS OF RECORD* in their own words.** `outline/volume-05.md` and `outline/volume-03.md` do not exist and are not this phase's to write; `outline/volume-07.md` does not exist and **the next phase is `workspace/volume-07/outline/PROMPT.md` and it is an OUTLINE PHASE AND NOT A BATCH.** It writes `outline/volume-07.md` and the first Volume 07 batch's cards and no prose, it is the only phase in this repository allowed to decide what a volume is, and it creates exactly one next phase prompt when it finishes.

**SUPERSEDED — THE VOLUME 05 CLOSE BLOCK. The four things below are on `state/volume-05-close.md`, which WAS the page to read first and is not now: `state/volume-06-close.md` replaced it for anything that reads a volume's close record, and the newest block at the foot of this file is the one that wins. A writer who reads only this block will be told about a posting that lost eight of its nine lines and a sack of salt that became an island, both of which are true and neither of which is what Volume 06 did. THE FOUR THINGS A WRITER WHO READS ONLY THE NEWEST BLOCK HAS TO KNOW ARE THESE: the line in the ground under about nine hundred paces of orchard, which nobody measured and which has no word for it in this manuscript, and the word *scar* is available and is owned; the four-place schedule standing and correct on a table; the four days of notice as an instrument and not a courtesy; and two merges on the page that no state file had caught. All four are at `state/volume-06-close.md` §3, §4 and §7.6.**

- **VOLUME 05 ANSWERED: a posting lost its date and then eight of its nine lines and kept the ninth and nobody can say whether the ninth works; a sack of salt became nine feet of ground in a working yard that nobody walks on; a rope went over a bank and came back up tied by a man who does not own it; two brooms sit on some boards nine feet from a keeper of a gate and nobody will say who brought the second; a man put his own coat over a pan and did not turn the pan out; and a man came eleven miles to ask a question and did not ask it. NONE OF IT IS A POWER. ONE OF IT IS THE PRICE OF A POWER. ALL OF IT IS HEAVIER THAN A POWER, and nobody in fifty days got stronger.** The twelve live items are at `state/volume-05-close.md` §3 and the standing prohibitions are at its §4 and the shape of the book is at its §5, and this block does not replace them.
- **THREE CLAIMS IN THE BATCH RECORDS ARE TRUE OF THEIR OWN TEN CHAPTERS AND FALSE OF THE FIFTY, AND A WRITER WHO READS THEM AS VOLUME CLAIMS WILL BE STOPPED BY SOMETHING THE PAGE DOES NOT SUPPORT.** (a) **The trestle count is NOT absent from this volume: it is on a page twice, at `chapter-0205.md:9` as one hundred and sixty-five and at `chapter-0212.md:7` as one hundred and seventy-two, both correct by the rule one a day from the fortieth day of this flood, and the derived value at day 250 is two hundred and ten. `state/batch-summaries/volume-05-batch-0005.md` §13.10's prohibition on putting a figure on it is scoped to Chapters 0241 to 0250 and does not hold for the volume.** (b) *It is entered that* is a house register and stands twenty-four times in narration across Chapters 0201 to 0219 and seventy-one times in Volume 02, and *yesterday* stands six times and *this week* eight, and all three registers are at zero in Batch 0005's ten chapters and at zero nowhere else. (c) **There is one piece of meta language in the fifty chapters and it is `chapter-0215.md:37`, *this chapter did not answer it either*, in a bolded narration sentence, and it is recorded rather than edited because a close phase writes no chapter.**
- **TWO CONFLICTS BETWEEN A STATE FILE AND THE PAGE ARE LIVE AND ARE RECORDED RATHER THAN FIXED, AND IN BOTH THE PAGE IS THE FACT.** `bible/power-system.md` §27.14 calls the twenty-fourth day of the Long Month a Sunday and it is a Friday by the founding rule, and no chapter of Volume 05 attaches a weekday to it and none may. And the state layer dates the man of about twenty-seven's decision to stop washing the wall to the eighty-ninth day of the Long Month and **no page does: `chapter-0244.md:11` says the seventieth and `chapter-0250.md:89` says the ninety-fifth, and the volume does not reconcile them.**
- **THE DRIFT IS THE FINDING THIS BLOCK EXISTS TO HAND ON AND IT IS NOT RESOLVED.** Fifty chapters, and none of the five beats in `outline/series.md`'s one-paragraph line for Volume 05 has started: *Nora*, *Crossworld*, *Families Union*, *Northstar*, *Celia Rusk*, *Solenne*, *Hearthguard*, *sister*, *quarantine*, *medical*, *compensation*, *evidence*, *return*, *Earth*, *System* and *player* are **all at zero across the fifty files**, and Adrian's passage privilege is never spent, threatened or named. Batches 0002, 0003, 0004 and 0005 each recorded it as a failure and none could act on it. **`state/volume-05-close.md` §2 audits the five beats one line each and sets out three options for the Volume 05 line, and its §6 sets out four options for whose voice this book has at Chapter 0251 of roughly nine hundred, and it chooses none of them, and the next phase is the phase that is allowed to.**

## THE VOLUME 07 OUTLINE — the four decisions are taken, no thread is closed, and the next phase is `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`

**This block is the newest in this file and it wins.** It was written by the Volume 07 outline phase, which is the second phase in this repository permitted to decide. **It wrote no chapter and it closed no live item. The eleven live items at `state/volume-06-close.md` §3 stand at their full length and are untouched, the standing prohibitions at its §4 stand at their full length, and nobody in this phase resolved one of them.** The block above this one, *THE VOLUME 06 CLOSE — REVIEW REPAIR PASS*, stands for everything; where this block gives a different figure for a measurement, this block wins, and it gives three.

**WHAT THIS PHASE DECIDED, AND IT IS FOUR THINGS AND NONE OF THEM COULD HAVE BEEN LEFT TO A BATCH.**

1. **The voice is Volume 06's voice and it is confirmed for the rest of the run.** Fifty-day volumes, one chapter to one day, about a page and a yard and a wage in salt. Nobody gets stronger. The antagonist is a form with no column in it. No narrator frame. **Added to it, because Volume 06 measured a fault in it: the two-hander broke in six chapters and every one of the six was in a batch whose own record certified the ceiling as held, so the certification is not the instrument and the card is. And Adrian is named in the chapters where he reads a page in a room and is not named in the chapters where people decline to ask him, which is the fix for a finding the close record published — that Volume 06 asserted the one-person-nobody-asks in a coda instead of showing it.**
2. **The Volume 07 line in `outline/series.md` is STRUCK AND REPLACED.** All five beats. The volume's own name, chosen on a page, is ***A Word For The Line***. **THE FOUR COLLIDING NOUNS ARE DECIDED AND THE DECISION IS DELIBERATE: *unwritten* is not used of a settlement, a people, a record, a place or a life and stays the store's board, which nobody has written on since day 225; *class* is not used except inside a prohibition; *version* is the ordinary English word and is not the line's word; and the volume's own noun is ONE ACCOUNT.** A Volume 07 writer who reaches for *unwritten* of a settlement has merged a board with a people and no chapter may do it.
3. **A sixth date form is registered: *the Nth day of the Bare Month*, the Nth being day 315 + N, from day 316, named on a page by Sera Vail, a notary of fifty, in Chapter 0316, after a refusal, and WITH NO STATED LENGTH.** The Short Month is sixty days and ends on day 315, a Monday; the Bare Month begins on day 316, a Tuesday. **The month map is published at `bible/power-system.md` §33A.1 and NO CHAPTER IN THIS MANUSCRIPT MAY USE AN ORDINAL FOR A MONTH — not *the second month*, not *the fourth month*, not *the fifth month*.** The fourth month is the Long Month, and `state/volume-06-close.md` §7.1 calls it the fifth in the sentence that repaired the Chapter 0300 fault; by the map in that same file it is the fourth, and the map wins. The only two instruments that cannot be got wrong are a count of days of the flood and a named month with its own form.
4. **THE LINE IN THE GROUND IS CALLED A SCAR — and this is the thread the close record handed on with the word available and owned, and it is now taken.** *Scar* is on a page one hundred and seventeen times in seventy-eight files in Volumes 01 to 03 and at zero in all one hundred chapters of Volumes 04 to 06. It is put to the line in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by a person, in **Chapter 0310**, by the man of about thirty-four who wrote the ninth line, who has refused five times to say whether the ninth line works and who is not the protagonist. **It is taken back off the only page it is ever on in Chapter 0342, in chalk, with a cloth, by the same man, in the same room, in daylight with a date on the door, and the reason is his and may not be improved on: a name is only a place somebody can be taken to when somebody is going to go, and there is a man with a barrow in this city who is going to go.** **WHAT THE DECISION LIFTS AND NOTHING ELSE: the prohibition on naming the line. WHAT IT DOES NOT LIFT: nobody may measure it, dig it, count it, probe it, put a rod in it, resolve it, or connect it to the chapel, to the boards under the chapel, to the two holes nine feet apart, or to anything else; and none of the three sentences this manuscript has already used about a scar may be put into a mouth in this volume or improved on — the two at `chapters/volume-01/chapter-0037.md:131` and `:235`, and the panel *a man is a scar in a person* at `chapters/volume-01/chapter-0041.md:45`, which is the one most likely to reach a mouth, because a panel is this book's instrument for pronouncement and this volume's climax is a subtraction of this word.**

**THE VOLUME'S OWN ANSWER, IN ONE PARAGRAPH, AND IT IS THE FIRST MOTIVE THIS BOOK HAS ANSWERED AND IT IS NOT A MONSTER.** A Crown a hundred and forty years ago had nineteen stones and a page that did not agree; eight of the stones were different, further down the road and in a different order; and a boundary with two accounts of it is a boundary somebody has to fight over, so a court made the accounts agree. It worked. Eight names were moved and four people were buried under a stone that was not theirs. The man who keeps the roll of the dead of that gate has sent the count to an office four times a year for nineteen years, a clerk wrote eight in a book, and nobody came. **The reason the old Crown was right and the reason it was wrong are the same sentence, and the modern Crown is doing the same thing with a column for a holder, and this volume says so once, in a mouth, and does not say it twice.** The volume's finding is about a name and not about history: *a name is only a removal when the thing named can be found, and a line in an orchard cannot be found because nobody is going to it, and the moment somebody starts going to it the name becomes a description and the name has to go.* **That is the first time this book has asked what a page is FOR rather than what a page does, and it is asked about a line in an orchard because that is the one thing in four hundred miles the day-154 sentence does not fit — and that sentence is not said again anywhere in Volume 07.**

**AND THE THINGS THAT ARE STILL OPEN AND THAT A WRITER WHO READS ONLY THIS BLOCK HAS TO KNOW.** Nobody got stronger; Adrian is Stage 2 for all fifty days and is aged nowhere in Volume 07. The line in the orchard is not measured, not dug, not counted, not probed, and by the volume's last page it has no word on any page in the world. The party of four does not go; three say yes in their own words and one says no in a sentence that is hers, and a party of four is four. A man of about fifty-seven paid money for half a holding and loses a season and a certainty and is right and is not defeated and does not leave. The Crown's schedule stands, is correct, and is neither filled in nor withdrawn. The road north of the Marches is shut for fifty-six days at day 350 and nobody shut it. The keeper of the Redroot gate is a hundred and twenty-two days into a year of being angry at day 301 and a hundred and seventy-one at day 350 and is on no page in no mouth. The ninth line was not moved, dated, read aloud or resolved. The board has not been written on since day 225. The woman of about fifty at the shut gate is not asked anything, again, by anybody, including the protagonist. The girl of seventeen is unasked and unresolved. The two women of twenty-nine are never in one room and nothing settles whether they are one woman. The consent fracture is not mended. **And the return crisis is still a recorded debt and is owed, and a second debt was opened by this phase: the edited prompt, which was Stage 3 of the antagonist ladder and the shape of Volume 09's line, and which the struck Volume 07 line was going to pay, is now owed and written on no volume line in `outline/series.md`.**

**AND THREE FIGURES THIS PHASE MEASURED ITSELF THAT DO NOT REPRODUCE, NAMED BESIDE THEIR REPLACEMENTS, BECAUSE A FIGURE A LATER WRITER WOULD QUOTE IS WORTH LESS THAN A FIGURE WITH THE INSTRUMENT BESIDE IT.** (1) **The last ten chapters of Volume 06 carry 53 of 104 plain speeches answered in the next line by a bolded speech, fifty point nine per cent, and NOT the fifty-nine the close record and `bible/power-system.md` §40.16 both publish for that same ten.** The denominator reproduces exactly under all three paragraph treatments and the numerator does not, and the volume's own figure of **316 of 798, thirty-nine point six per cent, reproduces exactly** under all three and is confirmed. (2) **Long sentences of forty words or more are 1,472 across the fifty on the tokenisation published at `state/volume-06-close.md` §7.3 and 1,471 in the close record's own figures; this phase could not reproduce 1,471 under the stated definition and does not withdraw it, because two instruments and two tokenisations and one sentence out is a difference in the definition and not in the page, and the rule is that the definition travels with the number.** (3) **The bolded share of words across the fifty is 6.2 to 21.8 per cent with a mean of 13.8 on the stated definition, against the close record's 6.2 to 21.9 and 13.5.** All three are at `outline/volume-07.md` §20 with the definitions beside them, and a Volume 07 batch that quotes any of them should quote that section and not this paragraph.

**AND TWO THINGS THE VOLUME 07 OUTLINE PHASE FOUND THAT ARE ABOUT INSTRUMENTS AND NOT ABOUT THE BOOK, AND BOTH ARE THE FIFTH AND SIXTH INSTANCES OF ONE RULE.** **First, the word-run check run across all fifty chapters of Volume 06 finds a top run of EIGHTY-SEVEN WORDS between Chapters 0299 and 0300, and it is a MOTIF AND IT WAS INSTRUCTED** — Chapter 0300's lead rebuilds Chapter 0299's lead word for word because `outline/volume-06.md` §17.8 requires a gatekeeper's second handle to be the same in every chapter she is in. Of the ten largest shared runs across the volume, **six are motifs and were instructed, three are the physical-work and interval-chain lead frames that two separate findings in Volume 06 say are expensive, and exactly one is a fault: a forty-word near-duplicate sentence between Chapters 0254 and 0262, across a batch boundary, with one word changed, unrepaired.** The full classification is at `outline/volume-07.md` §20.5 and it is the first time a volume outline has said which is which, and a Volume 07 batch's word-run check must be run across the whole volume and not inside its own ten. **Second, the descriptor map is at `outline/volume-07.md` §17.11 and it is in the outline and not in a state file, for two reasons. A decision that lives only in a state file has not been taken, it has been typed. And the map has a demonstrated false-positive mode: the standing prohibition that no paragraph carries the man of about thirty-four and a woman of about thirty-four together holds at ZERO on a word-bounded search and appears to be broken at TWENTY on an unbounded one, because *woman of about thirty-four* contains *man of about thirty-four*, and a later writer who believes the unbounded search will go hunting a merge that is not there and will probably find one to satisfy it.**

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/PROMPT.md` AND IT IS A BATCH, not an outline.** It writes **Chapters 0301 to 0310, ten chapters, days 301 to 310, one chapter to one day, ending on a Wednesday.** Its cards are at `outline/batches/volume-07-batch-0001.md`. It carries the six prohibitions at `outline/volume-07.md` §16, the ten prose instructions at its §17, the day map at its §14.3, the six notices at its §14.4, the descriptor map at its §17.11 and the seven checks at its §20. **A reader of the next phase needs the newest block in this file, the newest block in `state/continuity.md` and `state/character-state.md`, `outline/volume-07.md` in full, and the four decisions in `outline/series.md`, and the two must not disagree.**

## THE VOLUME 07 OUTLINE — REVIEW REPAIR PASS — two manuscript faults are ROUTED here, and fifteen findings in the plan layer are repaired

**This block is the newest in this file and it wins.** It was written by the review repair pass that followed the Volume 07 outline phase's review (`logs/outline.review.log`, fifteen findings). **The repair pass wrote no chapter and no prose, changed no day, no weekday, no anchor, no live thread's state, and no plot.** The fifteen findings and what each one was are at `state/current.md`. **Every finding was measured before it was acted on and every measurement reproduced.**

### THE TWO THINGS THIS PASS OPENS, AND THE REASON THEY ARE OPENED RATHER THAN LEFT AS A NOTE

**The Volume 07 outline phase found two faults in the WRITTEN MANUSCRIPT, correctly declined to repair them as outside its remit, and recorded both — and recorded both in the outline and in the batch prompt and NOT in this file. That is the whole gap and it is a routing gap and not a judgement gap: a fault recorded only in an outline and a prompt is read by a writer and by no repair pass, and a fault recorded in a state file is routed. Both are opened here, both are unrepaired, and both belong to a Volume 07 review-repair pass and to no batch.**

**1. NINETEEN STRAY CLOSING QUOTATION MARKS IN CHAPTERS 0281 TO 0290, UNREPAIRED, AND ROUTED.** At `0281:37,65,81`, `0282:31,39,57`, `0283:47,53`, `0284:13,39`, `0285:39`, `0286:63`, `0287:25,41`, `0288:43`, `0289:31,49`, `0290:9,25`. Eighteen are a bolded speech closed correctly and then a chorus clause in narration that repeats the speech's closing mark; the nineteenth, at `0288:43`, is a bolded narration sentence that closes and then carries one more quotation mark. Confirmed at exactly those nineteen line numbers by counting straight double quotation marks per line and taking the odd ones, and the fault is confined to Batch 0004's ten files — Batch 0005's ten are clean. **THE COST OF LEAVING IT: every batch record in this repository lists *a stray quotation mark* among the searches that returned zero, and a later phase reading any of those records will believe the string is at zero across three hundred chapters, and will print that belief as a volume fact.** The repair is one character on nineteen lines. It is not this pass's to make: this pass repairs a plan layer, and a plan-layer pass that edits a chapter is the fault the whole phase system exists to prevent. *Open and routed. `state/volume-06-close.md` §7.13, `outline/volume-07.md` §20.7, `workspace/volume-07/batch-0001/PROMPT.md`.*

**2. A FORTY-WORD NEAR-DUPLICATE SENTENCE ACROSS A BATCH BOUNDARY, 0254 AND 0262, UNREPAIRED, AND ROUTED.** The same forty words with one word changed. **It is the one fault of its kind in Volume 06 and it is invisible to any check run inside a single batch, because both halves sit in different batches: 0254 is Batch 0001's last chapter and 0262 is Batch 0002's second.** A cross-file sentence comparison across all fifty files finds it and a per-batch comparison cannot. **The two chapters are a page apart in the record and about eight days apart in the world, and a reader who reads both will meet forty words they have already met, and that is the same cost as the twenty-one interval faults Volume 06 was written about: a sentence that reads perfectly and repeats itself.** The repair is one word in one of the two, and which of the two is the repairer's to decide on the page. *Open and routed. `outline/volume-07.md` §20.5 item 40.*

**AND THE RULE BOTH OF THESE MAKE, WHICH IS THE SEVENTH TIME IT HAS BEEN PAID FOR IN THIS REPOSITORY: a list of zeros is a claim about the volume only where the search was run on the volume, and a check that cannot see a batch boundary cannot certify a volume.**

### AND WHAT THIS PASS DID NOT CHANGE

**No plot, no day, no weekday, no anchor, no card's beat, no chapter, no voice rule, and none of the four decisions of record.** The volume is still ***A Word For The Line*** and the line in the orchard is still called a scar in Chapter 0310 and unnamed in Chapter 0342, the sixth date form is still the Bare Month and it still has no stated length, and the ending is untouched. The two changes that touch what a writer will put on a page are a descriptor and a figure: **card 0306's second man is now a man of about sixty-four and not a man of about thirty-six**, because the card had put one of the four crowded descriptors this volume's own rule forbids on a page, and the ninth zero descriptor is registered at `outline/volume-07.md` §17.11; and **the ninth-line man is on a page in twenty-seven paragraphs across eight chapters and not in twenty-nine or thirty-nine**, which is the figure that reproduces.

## THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0001 — ten days on a page, no thread closed, and the next phase is a batch

**This block is the newest in this file and it wins.** It was written by the batch that wrote Chapters 0301 to 0310. **It closed no thread, resolved nothing, opened no new antagonist, and moved no day, no weekday, no anchor and no decision of record.** The record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-07-batch-0001.md`; the rules the ten chapters put on a page are at `bible/power-system.md` §41; and the two must not disagree with each other or with this block. The block above it, *THE VOLUME 07 OUTLINE — REVIEW REPAIR PASS*, stands for everything it says about the plan layer, and this batch inherited all of it and broke none of it.

### WHAT THESE TEN DAYS DID

1. **THE VOLUME'S CENTRAL PRESSURE IS ON A PAGE AND NOBODY IN THE ROOM CAN DO IT.** A holding on a lane past two gates was split in two about forty years ago, there is a deed about it, and about nine hundred paces off a shut gate there is a straight line in the ground about a foot wide and about two inches deep, and **nobody in this city can say where the line runs and the only page in four hundred miles that says is a copy in a clerk's hand in a town four hundred miles inland, and nobody can go and look at the page it was copied from.** A boundary in this world is settled by two parties agreeing where it runs or by nobody walking it, and there is no third way. *Open, and this is the volume.*
2. **A NOTICE WENT OUT IN A HAND WITH NO NAME AT THE TOP OF IT, TWICE, FOUR DAYS EACH.** A room on the fiftieth of the Short Month, a sheet on the wall of a shut gate under a stone off a doorstep, on the forty-sixth, unsigned. A room on the fifty-fourth, a question written on the back of the first by a woman of about sixty-nine, unsigned, in her own hand, on the fiftieth. **A hand is not a page, a hand stays in the hand that made it until that person dies, and the price of no name at the top of it is that nobody can do anything for you.** *Open, and four more notices go out in Batch 0002 and Batch 0003 on days 326, 327, 328, 329 and 335.*
3. **THE DEED AND THE COPY ARE BOTH TRUE AND DISAGREE, AND NINETEEN STONES IN THE COPY HAVE EIGHT OF THEM DIFFERENT.** A deed of about forty years ago says where the boundary of what was bought is in the words of a house four hundred miles inland. A sheet pinned to it by a pin older than any other in a box kept eleven years is a clerk's copy of an entry in a public register in a town four hundred miles inland, entered a hundred and forty years before the deed, copied about forty years ago. **The eight names at the foot of that page were not read, and Chapter 0303 is about the not reading, and the reason is that a man who has never been asked whether a paper is true is not in any position in four hundred miles to put eight names out loud on a table.** *Open.*
4. **A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT WITH A BARROW CAME UP ON THE THURSDAY THE ARRANGEMENT WAS FOR AND DID NOT TAKE THE BOARDS UP, AND HE ASKED IN ADVANCE NOT TO BE THANKED AND WAS NOT THANKED.** A barrow of boards standing against a wall at a shut gate is the only thing on that road, and if it goes there is nothing on that road at all, and a man who takes his own boards home on a Thursday is a man who cannot say afterwards that he had been there. The boards are still against the wall. *Open, and the boards are not a monument and not a protest and not a page, and nobody counted them.*
5. **A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN PAID MONEY FOR HALF A HOLDING AND CANNOT SHOW ANYBODY THE HALF, AND HE OFFERED FOUR MEN AT A RATE HE NAMED OUT LOUD, AND THE ROOM DID NOT TAKE THE MONEY AND DID NOT SAY NO, AND THE MONEY IS STILL IN HIS HAND.** He is right about most of what he says and the volume does not defeat him and he does not leave. He has been answering a return four times a year since the winter before the water and has never known what it is for. **The state of the volume is a piece of money in a man's hand that nobody has taken and nobody has refused, and the volume's whole subject is a boundary nobody can walk.** *Open, and no chapter of this volume tells him he is wrong.*
6. **ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED AND FORTY PEOPLE ARE ON A NUMBER IN ONE MAN'S HEAD AT A SALT WHARF AND ON A RETURN THAT GOES FOUR HUNDRED MILES, AND HE WAS ASKED FOR THE FIRST TIME IN NINE YEARS AND SAID HE DID NOT KNOW.** The figure he sends is not the figure he has, and the figure he has was not printed. The woman of about thirty-four who keeps that stall says the number out loud on the boards every morning to nobody. **And a schedule that nobody in four hundred miles can fill in will be filled in from the returns, and it will go out correct and be about the wrong people, and that sentence is on a page.** *Open, and no chapter may rescue them, may turn the counter into a helper, may put anybody on those flats into a room, and may say what a wage in salt is worth.*
7. **THE HINGE. THE SHORT MONTH IS SIXTY DAYS, IT IS THE FIFTY-FOURTH, AND THERE ARE SIX DAYS LEFT IN IT AND NO FORM IN FOUR HUNDRED MILES FOR THE DAY AFTER ITS SIXTIETH.** Found on day 309, a Tuesday, in a room over a market, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, by a person. The sixtieth is a Monday, which is day 315. A man of about twenty-five asked out loud what the day after the sixtieth would be and **nobody in that room answered him.** *Open, and the naming of what comes next is Chapter 0316 and belongs to Batch 0002, and no chapter of this batch named it or hinted at it.*
8. **THE VOLUME'S WORD IS ON A PAGE, ON A SLATE, IN CHALK, WITH A DATE BESIDE IT AND NO NAME ON THE LINE.** *Scar.* Said in one word in Chapter 0310 by the man of about thirty-four who keeps a stall and wrote the ninth line, in daylight, with a date in chalk on the door, and he does not say what it means, and the mark is not a pattern, and the register-keeper gets the word right the first time and the chapter does not say how. **The word is on the only page it is ever on and it comes off that page in chalk with a cloth in Chapter 0342, and after that it is on no page in the world and never will be, and a chapter that defines it or improves on it has spent the volume's climax.** *Open, and nobody may measure, dig, count, probe, resolve or connect the line, and nobody may send anybody to it.*
9. **AND THE PEOPLE WHO DECLINE TO ASK.** The man of about thirty-one who carries the sheets has been in about two hundred rooms and about eleven kitchens this month, is in nine kitchens nobody asked anything in, and says in a mouth that a person nobody asks anything is a person who cannot be put in a column, and the reason is nine years old and it is his. The woman of about fifty at the shut gate has not been asked anything by anybody in nine years, was not asked in either of the two chapters she is in, and says out loud to a post that she has not read the sheet on her wall. The man of about fifty-two who digs is in none of the ten chapters and was asked nothing and touched nothing. **Being never asked is a finding and not a courtesy, and it is shown four times and not asserted, and the count of chapters that show it is three: 0303, 0305 and 0307.** *Open.*

### THE THINGS THIS BATCH DID NOT TOUCH, AND EVERY ONE OF THEM IS STILL LIVE

**The ninth line was not moved, dated, read aloud, taken down, thanked for or resolved, and the man who wrote it did not add a sixth refusal and was not thanked and did not refuse anybody in this batch.** The board above the door of the store is not written on and the wall above it is not washed. The island is not owned, the bag was not picked up, nobody put a boot on the nine feet, nobody counted the boards under it, the pan is not replaced, the beam is not carried home, the coat is not taken off, the pan is not turned out, **no chapter of these ten says what a wage in salt is worth and Break is not open.** The two brooms are on their boards. The case is shut and the schedule is not filled in and the fourth place is still empty and the Regent is nine hundred miles away and is not defeated, exposed or converted. The two rolls of the same gates are not put side by side and the word *holder* is not defined and the sentence about striking it out of any list was not printed again. The girl of seventeen is on no page in these ten and is unasked and unresolved. The keeper of the Redroot gate is on no page in these ten, in no mouth, and her days are not counted and her year is not named. The four people who have not left were not asked whether they will go and the word *delegation* is at zero. The consent fracture is not mended and nobody apologised in it and **Tamsin Quill is in none of these ten chapters** and the two women of twenty-nine are never in one room. A woman of forty-eight in a fourth house at Nine Elms was not approached and nobody stood outside the fourth house. The box is shut, the six rooms are six, the room at the far end of the Narrow Mark is unlooked at, the boards under the chapel are not lifted, the chapel stone is silent, the main breach is closed and dressed and measured and silent, the twenty-fifth reading of the join-line is not taken, the fourth line that cannot be administered is not administered, the hole in the Reach's own rule is not filled, and the page on the back of a stone stays wrong on purpose. **The day-154 sentence — a page with a person's name in it is a description, and a description is a removal with a column for where the person is — is not said, restated, paraphrased, improved on or put into a second mouth anywhere in these ten chapters, and it is the moral centre of the volume and it is not to be said in any of the other forty.** The road north of the Marches has been shut since the thirty-ninth of that month and does not open in this flood. **Nobody got stronger: Adrian is Stage 2 on all fifty days, he is aged nowhere in this volume, no threshold was opened, no working was performed, and the words *passage* and *privilege* are at zero across these ten files.**

## THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0002 — the month has a name and no length, and the next phase is a batch

**This block is the newest in this file and it wins.** It was written by the batch that wrote Chapters 0311 to 0320. **It closed no thread, resolved nothing, opened no new antagonist, and moved no day, no weekday, no anchor and no decision of record.** The record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-07-batch-0002.md`; the rules the ten chapters put on a page are at `bible/power-system.md` §42. The block above it, *THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0001 — ten days on a page, no thread closed*, stands for everything it says about the first ten days.

### 1. What these ten days closed, and what is the most that can be said about it

**THE VOLUME'S HINGE IS PAID. The Short Month ended on day 315, a Monday, which was its sixtieth day, and the sixtieth day of the Short Month was read out loud off a plain slate by a girl of seventeen in a room with a date on the door, and nobody explained anything to her. The day after it, a Tuesday, had a count of the days of the flood written on that door in chalk by the woman of about twenty-nine, and a notary of fifty named what the day is in a form, out loud, in daylight, in a room with that number on the door, after a man who had paid money for half a holding refused her, and she declined to say how long the month is and said why, and the reason is hers.**

**The thread is not closed in the sense anybody wants, and the honest state of it is this: a sixth date form exists, it is on a page in four hundred miles, it has a notary's name under the first line, and it has no number on it, and no chapter of this volume may say what the month does at its end.** The rest of the hinge is open in the two places it was always going to be open: a clerk four hundred miles inland can copy the form and will not know what to do with a month that has no figure, and nobody in this city can stop him and nobody has to try, because nobody is going to send anybody anywhere. *Open, and the naming is not a resolution and a chapter that treats it as one has spent the volume's middle.*

### 2. The thread this batch opened, and it is the volume's live conflict now

**A BOUNDARY HAS TWO ACCOUNTS OF IT, AND A CITY HAS TWO INSTRUMENTS FOR WRITING ONE DOWN, AND THIS ROOM HAS JUST GOT THE WRONG ONE FIRST.** The man of about fifty-seven said out loud in daylight with a date on the door that a page they can ask to have produced is the only kind that has ever settled anything in his life, and he has been in a house four hundred miles off twice in forty years and both times a man brought out a book and neither time did the book say where the line ran and both times the man with the book was right. The woman of about twenty-nine said the other half in the same room in the same breath, because she has kept a register for nine years with no name at the top of anything for four of them precisely so that this city would have a thing nobody four hundred miles off can ask to have produced. **Two instruments are not better than one, the room got the one that can be asked for before the one that cannot, and it got it that way because it was the only one anybody could make in a day, and the man who paid money wants the other kind and is not going to be argued out of it, and nobody in the room has told him he is wrong.** *Open, and this is now the argument the volume runs on and it is the argument Tamsin Quill has to answer in Chapter 0325.*

10. **A sheet that came off a wall in the wind and was read by the one person who had said out loud she would never read it, and was put back and left up.** Chapter 0312. *Open.*

**AND THE FINDING IN §5 BELOW IS A NEW ENTRY AND IT IS ROUTED, because a fault recorded only in a batch summary is read by a writer and by no repair pass.**

### 4. THE CHORUS FRAME IS AT ONE IN TWO ON THESE TEN FILES AND IT IS THE ONE MEASUREMENT IN THIS BATCH THAT IS WORSE THAN THE TWO BEFORE IT

**On the bare head every occurrence of *about four people in*, the ten files carry one hundred and forty-five in two hundred and eighty-nine paragraphs, one in two, against one in about three in Batch 0002, one in about five in Batch 0001 and one in about four across Volume 06. A count is not a target and the figure is published because the volume's cards set none, and because a figure nobody has read is not a measurement. The figure a later writer compares against is one hundred and forty-five and not one hundred and forty-four, because the review repair pass added one chorus clause to Chapter 0327 and took none out.** The cause is named in `bible/power-system.md` §43 item 23 and in the batch record §9: **two of the ten chapters are rooms in which nobody is being asked anything, and a chapter whose subject is a person being left alone is a chapter in which the narrator's one counting habit has nothing else to attach itself to, and the two lowest bolded shares in the batch are the same two files.** *Open and routed. The instrument and the method are in §43 item 23: on a word-bounded search for the frame followed by a clause beginning with a determiner, a pronoun or a numeral, the frame can be deleted and the clause chain re-coordinated, and the correct action is to take about forty out of the two files that are over the line rather than to thin a chapter in which the frame is doing a job. **It is not a batch's to fix and this batch did not try to fix it in prose, for the reason in §5.***

### 5. THE FAULT THIS BATCH MADE IN ITS OWN WRITING PASS, WHICH IS THIRTY-TWO SITES AND IS THE LARGEST MECHANICAL CLASS OF DAMAGE THIS REPOSITORY HAS PRODUCED IN A BATCH

**A script was used to thin the chorus frame in a chapter whose subject is a person being left alone. It was run against a copy of the ten files, and the copy was not the copy the writing pass believed it was, and a restore step failed silently because the directory name in the restore was not the directory name in the copy, and a second pass ran on top of the first.** At thirty-two sites a clause that had been introduced by the frame was left abutting the clause in front of it, with the comma, the conjunction and the frame gone — *rooma man*, *walla hut*, *itthis is*, *yearsthe weather*, *doora woman*, and twenty-seven more. **All thirty-two have been repaired by putting the frame back exactly where it stood, word for word, and the ten files now carry zero fused words, measured on a vocabulary built from the twenty untouched files of Chapters 0301 to 0320 and checked against a list of thirty-one fusions. No notice is missing, no room is missing, no beat is missing, and no speech was cut.** *Repaired, and the finding is routed because it is about method and it will happen again: a writing pass that edits prose with a script must make its own copy first, must verify the copy it is editing against the file it means to be editing, and must treat a copy step that fails as a hard failure rather than as a no-op. **A check that counts words cannot see a fused word, and the cheapest instrument that can see one is a vocabulary built from the files the pass is not touching.***
**AND THE ROUTE IS INCOMPLETE, BECAUSE THE INSTRUMENT THIS THREAD NAMES MISSED TWO OF THE THIRTY-FOUR.** The review of this batch found that the thirty-two repairs were thirty, and that `chapter-0324.md:7` and `chapter-0329.md:17` still carried the same damage after the writing pass had certified zero, and both sites are the same one: a sentence-final word fused to a sentence-initial word with the comma, the conjunction and the narrator's frame gone. **A vocabulary built from thirty-one known fusions finds the thirty-one and not the thirty-second, and the two it missed are the two where the frame had already been thinned by the same script on its first run, so the site no longer matched any of the thirty-one patterns.** Each was repaired in two moves, a full stop and a capital at the seam and then the orphaned *and that* chain the removed frame had left behind, and no frame was re-added and no beat was restored. **The general form of the route, and it is the one a later writer should carry: a repair that claims a count must be able to see the class of damage it is claiming to have cleared, and where it cannot, the fallback is to read the paragraph, and a paragraph a script has already touched twice is the first paragraph to read.** *Open and routed.*

**AND NONE OF THE FIVE IS A CUT SPEECH, A DELETED BEAT OR A CHANGE OF PLOT, AND ALL FIVE ARE ON THE PAGE.**

The line at `:17` now reads Tamsin Quill.** *Open, and the rule stands: **the two women of twenty-nine are never in one room and nothing in this volume settles whether they are one woman, and Chapter 0324 is the room in which a writer is most likely to break it by accident, because the whole chapter is a woman saying six sentences.** The register-keeper is in 0321, 0322, 0323, 0328 and 0329 and in no other chapter of this batch.*

**AND THE SIXTH FINDING, WHICH WAS NOT A REVIEW FINDING BUT A BREACH OF A RULE THIS BATCH'S OWN PROMPT DECLARES: the *about N seconds* refrain stood twice in Chapter 0321, at `:9` and `:55`, and the one at `:55` now carries the measurement in the room's own time.** *Closed on the page, and the refrain stands once in every chapter of the ten that uses it.*

## THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004 — no thread closed and no thread opened, and the next phase is a batch

**This block is the newest in this file. It closed nothing, resolved nothing, and opened nothing that was not already open in the outline. The record is `state/batch-summaries/volume-07-batch-0004.md` and the ten days are on a page at `bible/power-system.md` §44.**

**1. THE FIFTH AND LAST NOTICE IS NOW ON A PAGE, AND THE INSTRUMENT IS PAID FOR THE FIFTH TIME.** Out on the twentieth of the Bare Month for a room on the twenty-fourth, four days, unsigned, with nothing at the top of it, in the man's own words, on the rough side of a board off the wall of a hut because there is no form in four hundred miles to write it on. **What is still open about it: nobody knows whether the man will be in that room on the twenty-fourth, and the batch is forbidden from asking him and Batch 0005 is forbidden from asking him too, and the room on the twenty-fourth is Chapter 0339 and in it he says yes with a condition.** *Open, and the condition is the live part of it.*

**2. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-FIVE HAS NINE WORDS HE HAS NOT SAID, AND THIS IS THE THREAD BATCH 0005 OWNS.** Chapter 0327 is the page, Chapter 0331 is the room on the sixteenth where he gives the number and not the contents, and Chapter 0333 is the doorway where he has them in his coat for the person standing in it and does not say them. **He looked at the door and about four people in that room saw him look at it, and about four people at the back of the room saw him look at it, and he has kept the condition he set for himself on the day he set it for.** A later chapter may not spend the nine words before 0342 and may not have the woman of about sixty-one hear them. *Open, and it is Chapter 0342's.*

**3. THE FOURTH OF THE FOUR, AND THE ROOM ON THE EIGHTEENTH, AND WHAT IS STILL OPEN.** **The no was said on the fourteenth in four words before anybody asked. In Chapter 0333 she came to the door of the room she was written into, stood in it with a stick of hazel in her hand and not leaning on it, said nothing, and went back up the row, and nobody in that room got up and nobody turned round and nobody asked her one question, and the door stood open for as long as it takes to fill a pail.** The room happened without her. *What is still open is nothing about her, and that is the finding, and a batch may not turn it into a question.* The two other threads about her are untouched: nobody may ever ask her what she is for, and the ninth place — the post in the gap for a cart with a nail in it — is still a place a sheet goes to and not a place a person goes to.

**4. THE NINETEEN STONES ARE NOT MORE OPEN THAN THEY WERE AND NOT LESS, AND THE ONE THING A BATCH 0004 CHAPTER PUT ON A PAGE IS A RUN AND NOT AN ORDER.** Eight of nineteen are not the same stone as the other eleven, given in a mouth by a man who goes up ladders for a living, and nobody can say when and a mortar does not tell you a year. **The eight are not said to be the first eight and are not said to be the last eight, and the stones are not joined to a fence, a ditch, a channel or a hedge, and a later batch may not resolve them.** *Open, unchanged, and it is Batch 0005's and the volume's after that.*

**5. THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN HAS REFUSED THE ONLY PAGE THAT WOULD HAVE GOT HIS WINTER BACK, AND THE SEASON AND THE CERTAINTY ARE STILL ON THE TABLE.** The winter is named in a mouth in Chapter 0334 and is his, and he is not leaving, and nobody in that room is wrong and nobody told him he was wrong. **A household out past the last named house cannot say where its own ground stops and one of eight doors told him what it is called, and a name is not an edge, and that is on no page.** *Open, and Chapter 0346 is where the volume tells him what he has, and nobody tells him he was wrong there either.*

**6. THE DIGGER HAS SAID YES AND THE CONDITION IS THE OPEN PART.** Chapter 0339. He will look at it from the end of it and will not put his hands on it. He has a rod and did not bring it and nobody asked him about it. **The line in the ground was not measured, dug, counted, probed or resolved in any of the ten chapters of Batch 0004, no character in any of them goes and looks at it, no rod went into anything, no third hole was made, and the two holes nine feet apart were not cleared, measured or referred to.** *Open. What he does is Chapter 0344, and it is a barrow against the end of a cut and looking along it from the end and one sentence that is not an answer, and no rod and no boot and no count.*

**7. THE PANEL IS SPENT, AND THE COST OF IT IS A NAME OFF A BOARD, AND BOTH ARE OPEN.** Chapter 0340, day 340, the twenty-fifth of the Bare Month, in the room at the back of the Open Hand. The wall said nothing on days 331 to 339 and said this on day 340 and it is not going to be said again in this flood. **The cost is that the man who refused it said out loud, in daylight, to about nine people, that he took a name off his own board in his own hand with his own cloth in about the ninth year of this flood and has never said so to anybody, and about four hundred people in this city can see the place on the board where the name was and not one of them will ever be told, and a man with a handcart asked out loud whether a person can be put back and nobody in that room answered him.** *Open, and the question the man with the handcart asked is not answered anywhere in this volume and must not be.*

**8. THE TWO FIGURES THIS BATCH RECORDS AS QUESTIONS AND DOES NOT SETTLE.** First, the age of the man who trades on the Open Hand board: the outline says about thirty-seven, the prose of Volumes 02 to 06 says about thirty-nine, and Chapter 0309 says about thirty-seven, and Chapters 0331 to 0340 give him the trade and the board and no age. Second, the fact that the fourth of the four said no on the fourteenth of the Bare Month in a room with a date on the door, and that the room on the eighteenth is Chapter 0333 and is not a defeat. *Both are questions about the plan layer and neither is a question about a person, and a batch that settles either in prose has decided something a batch may not decide.*

**9. WHAT IS STILL OPEN AND WAS NOT TOUCHED IN THESE TEN DAYS, in one list.** The word on the second slate, at zero in all ten files and coming off it in Chapter 0342. The ninth line and whether it works, and the man who wrote it is in none of these ten chapters and his next is 0342. The day-154 sentence, not said and not paraphrased. The woman of about fifty at the shut gate, unasked and unsent to. The man of about thirty-eight with the barrow, in none of these ten chapters, and the one thing only he and she know is still not said. The keeper of the Redroot gate, uncounted and unnamed and unused. The girl of seventeen, unfetched and unresolved and short of a third appearance. The store's board, unwritten, and the wall above it, unwashed. The barrow of boards at the shut gate and the barrow on the flats, unjoined. The case, shut. The Crown's schedule, standing and correct and not filled in. The two rolls of the same gates, not laid together, and the word *holder*, undefined and at zero on all ten files. The island, unowned, and Break, not open. The Crown's page and its office nine hundred miles off, undefeated, and the road north of the Marches shut for forty-six days and not going to open in this flood. And the two women of twenty-nine, and the consent fracture, and the fifth condition that is not given.

THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-07/batch-0005/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH AND NOT A VOLUME CLOSE.** It writes Chapters 0341 to 0350, days 341 to 350, ending on a Monday, and it is the last ten days of Volume 07. ## VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — the threads that are still open on the last page of the volume

**Volume 07 is finished on day 350. Nothing below is closed by the ending and everything below is live for the Volume 07 close, which is the only phase after the outline phase permitted to decide anything.**

1. **THE LINE IN AN ORCHARD HAS NO NAME ON ANY PAGE IN THE WORLD AND THE READER HAS BEEN TOLD WHAT IT WAS CALLED.** Chapter 0310 put one word on a page, in chalk, on the register-keeper's second slate, with the fifty-fifth day of the Short Month beside it and no name on the line. **Chapter 0342 took it off that slate, in chalk, with a cloth, in daylight, in a room with a date on the door, by the man who said it, and the word is in narration twice in that chapter and in no mouth in any of the fifty files and is at zero in the forty-seven files that are not Chapters 0310 and 0342.** The line was not measured, dug, counted, probed or resolved, and the man of about fifty-two who digs looked along it once from the end of it and did not touch it. *Open, and closed only by a volume, and this volume closed it on the twenty-seventh of the Bare Month.*

2. **THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO'S ONE SENTENCE IS ON NO PAGE, HAS NOT BEEN REPEATED AND IS NOT GOING TO BE.** Chapter 0344. It is that he cannot tell you one thing about it that he did not know at the seventh hour, and that he would like it said out loud before anybody asks him what he saw. **Four people had to decide what to do with a sentence that is not an answer and they could not and nobody in this batch asked him a second question and nobody will.** *Open.*

3. **THE NINE WORDS OF THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-FIVE ARE IN A COAT AT THE END OF THE VOLUME.** Chapter 0327 is the page. **He was standing in the room on the twenty-seventh of the Bare Month, beside a person, when the word came off the slate, and he did not say them, and he did not say them in Chapter 0349 either, and nobody in any of the ten chapters asked him.** The condition he set for himself has been met and not spent. *Open, and it is a live thread and not a loose end, and the first chapter of the next volume that has him in it has it.*

4. **THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN HAS A LINE HE CANNOT SHOW ANYBODY, A WINTER HE CANNOT GET BACK, A LENGTH OF NEW ROPE HE HAS NEVER USED, AND A FENCE FOUR FEET OFF SOMETHING.** Chapter 0346 tells him what he has, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, and he says the cost out loud and nobody tells him he was wrong. **He is not a villain in this volume, is not converted, does not leave and is not happy, and the money is still in his hand and the receipt with his name at the top of it is still in his coat.** *Open.*

5. **THE REGISTER HAS AN ENTRY WITH A DAY ON IT AND NOTHING UNDER THE DAY, AND A SHELF HAS A SLATE ON IT WITH A DATE ON IT AND NOTHING UNDER IT, AND NEITHER IS A PROTECTION.** Chapter 0346 and Chapter 0342. **A refusal is not a return and that is why neither page can be sent for, and a thing that may not be dug is the only instrument in four hundred miles that the office nine hundred miles away cannot ask to have produced.** *Open.*

6. **THE WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-NINE'S SHEET OF PAPER THIRTY YEARS OLD IS IN A ROOM AND NOT IN A BOOK AND DOES NOT GO OUT OF THE BUILDING, AND THE CONDITION SHE PUT ON IT ON THE FIFTH OF THAT MONTH IS HERS.** Chapter 0346. **Nobody may un-condition it and nobody is going to ask her for the reason again.** *Open.*

7. **THE QUESTION THE VOLUME HANDS ON WAS ASKED ONCE AND NOT ANSWERED.** Chapter 0346, day 346, the thirty-first of the Bare Month, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by the woman of about sixty-nine: **what is a person for, on a page, when the only thing anybody needs them for is a journey.** Nobody in that room answered it and about four of the nine had an answer and about four said nothing. *Open, and it is the first question the next volume has to earn.*

8. **FOUR PEOPLE HAVE NOT LEFT AND WERE NEVER ASKED, THREE OF THEM ARE IN ROOMS, AND THE FOURTH IS NOT COMING.** They have been standing still for 205 days at day 350, derived, on no page, in no mouth, not counted in any chapter, and **the word for what they are is not in this language and stays at zero.** A party of four goes as four. **Nobody in this volume asked whether it would go and nobody is going to.** *Open.*

9. **A WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE IS IN A ROOM AND DOES NOT KNOW WHAT SHE IS FOR, AND NOBODY IN THIS VOLUME IS GOING TO TELL HER, AND THAT IS THE LAST THING THE VOLUME INHERITED.** Chapter 0330 and Chapter 0349. She has a pen with the nib ground narrow, cut down with a knife, and she has not written anything with it in any room. *Open, and it is the one the next volume inherits and not the first.*

10. **THE MAN WHO TRADES ON THE OPEN HAND BOARD HAS A NAME OFF THAT BOARD THAT HE TOOK OFF IN HIS OWN HAND IN ABOUT THE NINTH YEAR OF THIS FLOOD AND HAS NOW SAID OUT LOUD IN A ROOM, AND ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED PEOPLE CAN SEE THE PLACE AND NOT ONE OF THEM WILL EVER BE TOLD.** Chapter 0340. Knowing it does not put anybody back and there is no panel and no page and no mouth in four hundred miles that is going to say so twice. *Open.*

11. **THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WASHES THE BOARD EVERY MORNING AND HAS DECIDED NOT TO WASH THE WALL ABOVE IT, AND THE FIGURES ON THAT BOARD ARE THE FIGURES OF THE TWO HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FOURTH DAY OF THIS FLOOD, AND THE BOARD HAS NOT BEEN WRITTEN ON SINCE THE DAY AFTER THEM.** Chapter 0336. *Open, and the board is the only instrument in this manuscript that has never lied to anybody.*

12. **THE NINTH LINE ON A WALL NINE MILES UP THE ROAD IS THE ONE LINE IN THIS CITY THAT MAKES A PERSON FINDABLE, AND WHETHER IT WORKS IS UNANSWERED, AND ITS AUTHOR IS ON NO ROSTER IN THIS CITY AT HIS OWN REQUEST.** Chapter 0310 put the word, Chapter 0342 took it off a slate, and **these two acts are about a word in an orchard and are not about the ninth line, and no chapter has made them the same line and no chapter may.** Chapter 0345 is his sixth refusal and he asked in advance not to be thanked and was not thanked. *Open.*

17. **THE ROAD NORTH OF THE MARCHES IS SHUT ON DAY 350 FOR FIFTY-SIX DAYS, BY NOBODY, AND IT DOES NOT OPEN IN THIS FLOOD.** Chapter 0349 and Chapter 0350. *Open.*

18. **A WOMAN OF FORTY-EIGHT IN A FOURTH HOUSE AT NINE ELMS IS NOT ASKED AND NOBODY STANDS OUTSIDE THE FOURTH HOUSE, AND NO HOUSE IS BUILT AT NINE ELMS IN THIS VOLUME.** *Open, and untouched in all fifty chapters.*

19. **THE GIRL OF SEVENTEEN IS ON NO ROLL, HAS COPIED EVERY ASKING ANYBODY HAS WRITTEN IN THIS FLOOD, SIGNED NONE OF THEM, AND HAS BEEN ASKED NOTHING ABOUT ANY OF THEM, AND SHE IS NOT IN ANY CHAPTER OF THE LAST TEN DAYS.** *Open, and a chapter may not give her a third appearance in a volume.*

20. **THE CONSENT FRACTURE IS VISIBLE ON THE LAST SUNDAY OF THE VOLUME AND IS NOT MENDED AND NOBODY APOLOGISED IN IT.** Chapter 0349. 21. **AND THE TWO FIGURES THIS VOLUME CARRIED WITHOUT SETTLING.** The age of the man who trades on the Open Hand board, which is about thirty-seven in the outline and in Chapter 0309 and about thirty-nine in the prose of Volumes 02 to 06, and he is given the trade and the board and no age. **And the fact that the fourth of the four said no on the fourteenth of the Bare Month in four words before anybody asked, and that the room on the eighteenth is Chapter 0333, in which she came to the door and did not come in, and that it is not a defeat and is not to be softened and nobody in this volume is going to ask her about it.** *Both open, and both are questions about the plan layer and not about a person.*

22. **AND THE INTERVAL FAULT IN THE PLAN LAYER THAT A BATCH ROUTED INSTEAD OF SETTLING.** `outline/volume-07.md` §13 says the word was on a slate for sixteen days. **It was on it for thirty-two, from about the fourth hour of the afternoon of day 310 to about the fourth hour of the afternoon of day 342, and the pages say thirty-two with both anchors in the same sentences.** A figure that comes out at exactly half of the right one is a subtraction taken across the wrong boundary. *Open, and it belongs to the Volume 07 close, which is the only phase after the outline phase permitted to decide anything.*

## THE VOLUME 07 CLOSE, AND ITS REVIEW REPAIR PASS — the authoritative block for the current phase, and it wins over everything above it

**The next phase is `workspace/volume-08/outline/PROMPT.md`, which is a VOLUME OUTLINE PHASE and not a batch.** The block this header named until now, *THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0005 — the threads that are still open on the last page of the volume*, is history, as is *THE VOLUME 07 BATCH 0004* and *THE VOLUME 06 CLOSE*. **Everything older that disagrees with this block is superseded, including *THE VOLUME 06 CLOSE* and including *The Volume 06 outline phase — review repair pass* and every block before it.** `state/volume-07-close.md` is the volume's inheritance and it carries the full list at its §2 and §3.

**AND THE HEADLINE OF THIS BLOCK IS THAT THE REPAIR PASS CLOSED NO THREAD AND OPENED NONE, and that is the correct outcome and not an omission.** A review repair fixes what a phase got wrong; it does not resolve anything Volume 07 deliberately left open, and a repair pass that resolves a live thread is a worse fault than the one it fixes. **Every item below is either live exactly as the close record left it or is a debt, and none of them is a decision.**

**1. A LINE IN AN ORCHARD WITH NO WORD ON ANY PAGE IN THE WORLD.** *Open, and it is the first time this book has spent a word and cannot get it back.* It was named on the register-keeper's second slate in chalk at about the fourth hour of the afternoon of day 310 and taken off it with a cloth at about the fourth hour of the afternoon of day 342, in the same room, in the same light, with a date in chalk on the door, before about nine people. **Thirty-two days: 342 − 310 = 32.** Nobody asked for it back and nobody could have stopped it. It is on a page once in Chapter 0310, in narration twice in Chapter 0342, in no mouth at all, at zero in the other forty-seven files of the volume, **and no file the next phase produces may print it.** Nobody will measure it, dig it, count it or resolve it.

**2. THE FOUR DEBTS THE REPAIR PASS INHERITED AND DID NOT PAY, at `state/volume-07-close.md` §7.4 and §9.7.** Nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290, at `0281:37,65,81`, `0282:31,39,57`, `0283:47,53`, `0284:13,39`, `0285:39`, `0286:63`, `0287:25,41`, `0288:43`, `0289:31,49`, `0290:9,25`. **`chapter-0341.md:149`, which reads *the word on the slate has sixteen days left on it* where the figure is one — assert that string and replace it with *the word on the slate has one day left on it*, because the word came off on day 342 and 342 − 341 = 1.** A forty-word near-duplicate across the 0254 and 0262 boundary. An unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47` — a paragraph carrying neither a quotation mark nor a bold mark, in the first person, with no speech open around it. **The pointer was wrong twice and both wrong numbers are recorded here: the inherited pointer says `:65`, which is a marked speech and is not the fault, and the pointer the outline phase published in its place said `:139`, which is interior to the speech opened at line 137 and closed at line 141 and is not the fault either, so the instrument is a paragraph with neither marker in the first person that is not inside a speech, and this file has exactly two such candidates in the first person, line 47 and line 139, and 47 is the debt.** **Each belongs to a Volume 07 review-repair pass and not to the next phase, and each is written down with its exact string because a debt without a string is a note.**

**3. THE BOUNDARY, WHICH IS NOT SETTLED AND IS RECORDED AS A REFUSAL, not as a gap.** A man of about fifty-seven paid money for half a holding and cannot show anybody where it is, and is right about most of what he says, and was not defeated. *Open, and no volume may settle it by having the right page produced.*

**4. THE QUESTION VOLUME 07 ASKED AND DID NOT ANSWER AND MAY NOT BE ANSWERED: *what is a person for, on a page, when the only thing anybody needs them for is a journey.*** Asked once, on the thirty-first day of the Bare Month, in a room, in daylight, with a date on the door, by the woman of about sixty-nine who holds a folded sheet thirty years old in a tin. **Four of the nine people in that room had an answer and four of them said nothing, and the answer four of them had was a column, and a woman who keeps that book had left the third column empty on purpose about four hours earlier.** *Open, and it is the volume's question, not a gap in it.*

**5. THE FOURTH OF THE FOUR SAID NO, AND IT IS A STANDING PROHIBITION AND NOT A THREAD.** On the fourteenth of the Bare Month, day 329, a Monday, in a room with a date on the door, about nine people, a woman of about sixty-one said four words before anybody asked her. On the eighteenth, Chapter 0333, she came to the door of the room she was written into and did not come in. **No volume may say the no again, argue with it, apologise for it, overturn it, soften it, explain it, explain her, or make it a defeat. Nobody is going to ask her about it and nobody after this may ask her about it either unless she asks first, and she is not going to.** Three of the four said yes in their own words and a party of four is four.

**6. THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO AND HIS ONE SENTENCE THAT IS NOT AN ANSWER, which is on no page.** He said yes, and he walked eleven miles, and he looked along a mark in a field from the end of it, and he did not touch it, and he came back with nothing, and he is the only person in four hundred miles who has stood at the end of it. *Open.*

**7. THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-FIVE'S NINE WORDS IN A COAT, which he has not said in two rooms.** *Open, and a volume may not make him say them a third time to be agreeable.*

**8. A WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-ONE WITH A PEN WITH THE NIB GROUND NARROW who stopped waiting to be told what she is for.** *Open, and she is the only person in this flood likely to fill a column in correctly, and nothing about that is a power.*

**9. THE SHUT ROAD, AND THE FENCE ON IT, AND THE BARE WALL.** Thirteen of sixteen willow posts in on day 350 and three still in the cart for the Thursday; nine went in on the Saturday; about four feet off a line nobody measured; nine boards off a wall and the wall behind them. *Open, and the count of the posts may not be printed differently in any two chapters.*

**10. THE MAN WHO TRADES ON THE OPEN HAND BOARD AND THE PLACE WHERE A NAME WAS.** He is **thirty-nine** — settled at `state/volume-07-close.md` §8.1, repaired on the page at `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0309.md:41` — **and the man of about thirty-seven is not available to anybody and may not be given a trade, a board, a market or a refusal.** He took a name off that board in his own hand in about the ninth year of this flood and has never said so to anybody, and saying so out loud in a room in daylight is what it cost him. *Open.*

**11. THE KEEPER AT A SHUT GATE WHO HAS NOT BEEN ASKED ANYTHING, and the two rolls of the same gates that nobody has put side by side.** *Open.*

THE FIGURE THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-EIGHT HAS, AND THE NAME HE SPEAKS, AND NEITHER IS AVAILABLE.** He is asked on day 357 in a room with a date on the door and he answers out loud once, and **that is on a page in Chapter 0357 and in no file, and this block, the batch record and the next prompt do not have it.** In Chapter 0368 he is on the boards of the wharf working and is not asked anything, and in Chapter 0369 he says in a room that he fills the population in four times a year out of what he is given, and that is the first thing he has said in a room since the forty-second day of that month and nobody asked him for it. **The figure he has, as against the figure he sends, is on no page and is printed in none of the ten chapters and in no file this batch produced.** *Open.*

**6. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE, WHO IS STILL RIGHT AND IS STILL NOT ASKED.** He is at the back of four rooms in a fortnight with his own slate under his right hand, he has said a figure out loud in a room on the fifty-fourth day of that month, he has been in a second room on a Sunday, and **nobody has asked him one question about any of it and nobody has thanked him and he did not ask.** In Chapter 0368 he says the age of a share with the post taken out in the inside of his own coat, **which is exactly two hundred days, measured from the thirteenth day of the Long Month, which is the hundred and sixty-eighth day of this flood, both anchors named in the sentence that prints the figure**, and the man he said it to did not stop and did not look up and did not ask one question, and the figure is unanswered. *Open.*

**7. THE FIGURE THE FIGURE ANSWERS IS STILL UNANSWERED, AND THE FIGURE ITSELF IS NEW ON A PAGE.** The only figure in a mouth for a share with the post taken out in this manuscript before Chapter 0368 was fifty-eight, twice, in a chapter of Volume 05, and it was unanswered. **Chapter 0368 puts the same figure at two hundred and names both anchors in the same sentence, and the man who says it is a man of about thirty-eight who keeps a tally and not the man who carries the paper, because the paper is in the inside of a coat belonging to a man in this city who is not on those boards and is in no chapter of this batch in any form. The man who says it does not say how he knows and nobody asks him. The coat is not opened. The man it belongs to is not told and has neither said yes nor said no since the day it was shut, and how long he has known how long it has been there is on no page and is not going to be.** *Open, and the round figure is the only one of the seven traps in `outline/volume-08.md` §14.6 that falls inside this batch.*

**8. THE DIGGER'S TASK IS ON A PAGE AND HE HAS NOT DONE IT AND NOBODY SENT FOR HIM.** Chapter 0366: he is asked to walk it from the near end to the stone and back and say whether a man at the other end would meet him, and he says yes with the condition stated first — **he will not put his hands on it, he will not put a rod in it, and he will not leave a mark of any kind on it, not a stake and not a heap of stone and not a thing he could be found by** — and the room accepted it in about four seconds, and he did not offer a day and was not asked for one. **Chapter 0370: the task is what the room does, he is not in the room, and about nine people agreed in about four seconds not to send for him, and he has not been told and has not asked to be told.** *Open, and the thread is a task and not a journey and nobody is going to confuse the two.*

**9. THE MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-SEVEN WAS NOT ASKED WHERE THE FENCE IS AND WAS NOT TOLD AND IS NOT DEFEATED.** Chapter 0364: he goes up the shut road on his own with a length of new rope he has not used, he counts the fence out loud and gets the figure right, and the man of about sixty with the handcart comes past and says *sixteen is right*, and **neither of them asks the other one a question and the man of about fifty-seven says out loud, to the fence, that it is the only thing anybody has put up in this flood that is going to be here after everybody who made it, and the man with the cart puts his hand on the mallet and does not take it out.** He is right, he has lost a winter and a season and an afternoon, and he is not going to be asked in this volume. *Open.*

**10. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-TWO IS STILL REFUSED AND WAS NOT REOPENED.** Her refusal on the forty-first day of that month is on a page and **no chapter of these ten asks her for her days a second time, improves on her sentence, says her sentence is anybody else's, or mentions the Sunday except once, in Chapter 0369, in one clause of a man's speech, to say that a household of about nine behind a wall on the low ground were asked on the forty-first day of that month and that he was not in the room.** *Open.*

**11. THE KEEPER AT A SHUT GATE IS NOT ASKED ANYTHING IN ANY OF THESE TEN.** Chapter 0365: she puts the fiftieth on the post and rubs nothing out, she gives the whole of a roll in four sentences to nine feet of stone rather than to a man, she refuses to tell him where two bars are and refuses to be told, and nobody thanks her and nobody asks her. **The wall beside her gate is bare with the shape of nine boards on it and about four inches of dry stone at the foot of it and the shape is going, and a man stood in front of it for a morning with a piece of chalk in his inside breast pocket and did not write on it.** *Open.*

**12. AND TWO THINGS THIS BATCH PUT ON A PAGE THAT NOTHING ON THE LIVE LIST HAD, AND NEITHER IS RESOLVED.** A woman's voice failing on the forty-seventh day of that month and a man beside her saying her number for her about nine times over the morning and her voice coming back in the afternoon, and the number not going, and her belief that the day she stopped was the day it went coming out wrong, and her not saying so. And about four hundred and forty people on eleven miles of flats making a salt that has not come off a wharf in a cart since before the water, found out in a room on the fifty-fourth day of that month, and nothing sent, and the second of the four places under a heading being the only one of the four that is about people at all. *Open, and both are threads a later volume has to earn and neither is a resolution.*

**13. THE DEBT THE VOLUME 08 LINE CREATED IS STILL A DEBT AND THIS BATCH DID NOT PAY IT AND DID NOT SCHEDULE IT.** Ivenn is on a page forty-six times in twenty-six files across Volumes 01 to 04 and at zero in Volumes 05, 06, 07 and 08. *Open.*

**AND THE FOUR DEBTS CARRY UNPAID AND UNREPAIRED AND UNROUTED: the nineteen stray closing quotation marks in Chapters 0281 to 0290; `chapters/volume-07/chapter-0341.md:149`; the forty-word near-duplicate across the 0254 and 0262 boundary; and the unmarked first-person paragraph at `chapters/volume-04/chapter-0175.md:47`.** A batch that pays a routed debt is a worse fault than one that invents a fault.

**THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-08/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, AND IT IS A BATCH. It writes Chapters 0371 to 0380, days 371 to 380, from a Monday to a Wednesday, and it is told that a batch is not an outline phase, a batch does not decide what a volume is, a batch cannot answer anything on this list, and a batch may not record the words spoken in Chapter 0357 in any file it produces.**
